<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">To show it again on desktop, open <strong>Hidden Bots</strong>, right-click the Bot, and choose <strong>Show in sidebar</strong>. On the phone, open <strong>Show Hidden Bots</strong> and choose <strong>Unhide</strong>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If it is missing from both lists, use the checks in <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/computer-recovery#my-chats-or-bots-look-missing-were-they-deleted">My chats or bots look missing</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-troubleshoot-grok-bot-computer-routine-connector-and-startup-problems"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-troubleshoot-grok-bot-computer-routine-connector-and-startup-problems" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-troubleshoot-grok-bot-computer-routine-connector-and-startup-problems" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-troubleshoot-grok-bot-computer-routine-connector-and-startup-problems"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I troubleshoot Grok Bot computer, routine, connector, and startup problems?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Most operational issues clear with a full restart. Fully quit Grok Bot (on Mac, choose <strong>Quit</strong> from the menu bar, not just closing the window), reopen, and try again before anything else.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If that doesn&#x27;t help, match your symptom:</p>
<div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead class="dark:text-neutral-300"><tr><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">Symptom</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">What to try</th><th class="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-left font-medium text-xs" scope="col">If it persists</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-background dark:divide-muted"><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Computer says <strong>Reconnecting</strong>, <strong>Couldn&#x27;t Reach Grok Bot&#x27;s Computer</strong>, <strong>Backup not ready</strong>, or <strong>Bot failed to respond</strong></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Wait and <strong>Retry</strong>, then fully quit and reopen. <strong>Update</strong> if offered. <strong>Recover</strong> if it stays unreachable or the update looks stuck.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/computer-recovery">Grok Bot computer</a>. Use <strong>Reset</strong> only last. Like <strong>Recover</strong>, it can lose very recent changes if the computer can&#x27;t be reached to save them.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Chats look gone, or bots vanished from the sidebar</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Fully quit and reopen. Check another device on the same account. Open <strong>Hidden Bots</strong>.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/computer-recovery#my-chats-or-bots-look-missing-were-they-deleted">My chats or bots look missing</a>. Don&#x27;t use <strong>Recover</strong> or <strong>Reset</strong> to look for them.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">A secret value is missing in Shell, or secrets look wiped after Update</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">A saved value is not shown again. If the name is still listed under <strong>Secrets</strong>, it is still stored.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/secrets">Store secrets securely</a>.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">A routine isn&#x27;t running</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Confirm the routine isn&#x27;t <strong>Paused</strong> and <strong>When to run</strong> shows the schedule you expect. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/routines#why-hasnt-my-routine-run">Why hasn&#x27;t my routine run?</a></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">If it isn&#x27;t paused but hasn&#x27;t run for 24+ hours with no error shown, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/troubleshooting/reporting-bugs">report a bug</a> with the agent name and routine details.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">An error or crash during a run</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Copy the request ID from the message, then try the task again. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#how-do-i-copy-a-request-id">How do I copy a request ID?</a>.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">If the same prompt fails on a fresh run, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/troubleshooting/reporting-bugs">report a bug</a> and include the error text and request ID.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">A plugin won&#x27;t authorize or shows disconnected</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Re-add the plugin and finish the provider login in your browser. If it shows <strong>Waiting for authorization</strong>, use <strong>Reopen</strong>. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#how-do-i-reconnect-a-plugin">How do I reconnect a plugin?</a></td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">If re-authorizing completes but the plugin still shows disconnected, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">contact support</a> with the plugin name and your account email.</td></tr><tr><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">The app hangs on launch</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Fully quit and relaunch. On Mac, use <strong>Quit</strong> from the menu bar.</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">If it hangs on every launch, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">contact support</a> with your platform and Grok Bot version.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">When you contact support or report a bug, include your Cursor account email, your platform and Grok Bot version, the agent name, the exact error text or a screenshot, and the steps you already tried. Copy the version from <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#how-do-i-find-my-grok-bot-version">About</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-copy-a-request-id"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-copy-a-request-id" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-copy-a-request-id" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-copy-a-request-id"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I copy a request ID?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Right-click the message and choose <strong>Copy request ID</strong>. You can also hover the message, open <strong>More message actions</strong>, and choose <strong>Copy request ID</strong>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Paste the full ID into the report. If <strong>Copy request ID</strong> is not in the menu, that message does not have one you can copy. Include the error text and a screenshot instead.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-find-my-grok-bot-version"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-find-my-grok-bot-version" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-find-my-grok-bot-version" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-find-my-grok-bot-version"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I find my Grok Bot version?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Open the account menu and choose <strong>About</strong>. The dialog shows <strong>Version</strong>, and <strong>Copy version info</strong> copies the version, release track, and OS. That is the version to send with a support request.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-update-grok-bot"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-update-grok-bot" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-update-grok-bot" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-update-grok-bot"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I update Grok Bot?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The desktop app checks for updates on its own. When one is ready, the account menu shows <strong>New update available</strong>. Choose <strong>Install</strong>, then <strong>Restart to update</strong>. Your Bots keep working in the cloud while the app restarts.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">To check yourself, open <strong>Settings</strong>, go to <strong>Updates</strong>, and choose <strong>Check for Updates</strong>. If an update is ready, choose <strong>Restart to Update</strong>. <strong>Automatic Updates</strong> installs updates while you&#x27;re away.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Phone updates come from the App Store or Google Play. Product changes are listed on the <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://cursor.com/changelog">changelog</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="why-does-grok-bot-say-update-required"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#why-does-grok-bot-say-update-required" data-heading-base-id="why-does-grok-bot-say-update-required" aria-label="Copy link to why-does-grok-bot-say-update-required"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Why does Grok Bot say Update required?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Grok Bot requires an update when your version is more than 14 days old and a newer version is out. The app shows <strong>Update required</strong> and says your version is no longer supported.</p>
<ol class="mb-4 prose-ol-markers" style="list-style-type:decimal;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Choose <strong>Update</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">When the download finishes, choose <strong>Restart to update</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If the update fails, choose <strong>Try again</strong>. You can also choose <strong>Download the latest version</strong> and install it yourself.</li>
</ol>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On Linux, the screen may show a command to run instead. Choose <strong>Copy command</strong>, run it in a terminal, then restart Grok Bot. The command depends on how you installed Grok Bot:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Debian or Ubuntu: <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">sudo apt update &amp;&amp; sudo apt install --only-upgrade grok-bot</code></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Fedora or other RPM systems: <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">sudo dnf upgrade --refresh grok-bot</code></li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If the screen asks you to download and install the latest package, do that, then restart Grok Bot.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On the phone, <strong>Update required</strong> has an <strong>Update Grok Bot</strong> button that opens the App Store or Google Play.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="grok-bot-closed-to-update-but-it-is-still-on-the-old-version-what-do-i-do"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#grok-bot-closed-to-update-but-it-is-still-on-the-old-version-what-do-i-do" data-heading-base-id="grok-bot-closed-to-update-but-it-is-still-on-the-old-version-what-do-i-do" aria-label="Copy link to grok-bot-closed-to-update-but-it-is-still-on-the-old-version-what-do-i-do"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Grok Bot closed to update, but it is still on the old version. What do I do?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<ol class="mb-4 prose-ol-markers" style="list-style-type:decimal;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Check the version. Open the account menu and choose <strong>About</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Fully quit Grok Bot and reopen it. On Mac, choose <strong>Quit</strong> from the menu bar, not just close the window.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Open <strong>Settings</strong>, go to <strong>Updates</strong>, and choose <strong>Check for Updates</strong>. Then choose <strong>Restart to Update</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">If the version still hasn&#x27;t changed, download the latest version from <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="https://cursor.com/dashboard/bot">cursor.com/dashboard/bot</a> and install it over the current app. Your Bots and chats belong to your account, so they are still there after you reinstall.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">On Linux, run the update command for your system. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#why-does-grok-bot-say-update-required">Why does Grok Bot say Update required?</a></li>
</ol>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If the old version keeps coming back, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">contact support</a> with the version from <strong>About</strong> and your platform.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-change-the-app-language"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-change-the-app-language" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-change-the-app-language" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-change-the-app-language"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I change the app language?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On desktop, open <strong>Settings</strong>, go to <strong>General</strong>, and choose a <strong>Language</strong> under <strong>Appearance</strong>. <strong>Follow System</strong> uses your computer&#x27;s language. Desktop has 31 languages: English, Afrikaans, Arabic, Bengali, Chinese (Simplified), Chinese (Traditional), Czech, Danish, Dutch, Finnish, French, German, Greek, Hebrew, Hindi, Hungarian, Indonesian, Italian, Japanese, Korean, Norwegian Bokmål, Polish, Portuguese, Russian, Spanish, Swedish, Thai, Turkish, Ukrainian, Urdu, and Vietnamese.</p>

<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On the phone, open <strong>Settings</strong> and choose <strong>Language</strong>. <strong>System</strong> uses your phone&#x27;s language. The phone app has English, Chinese (Simplified), Chinese (Traditional), French, German, Hindi, Japanese, Korean, Polish, Portuguese, and Spanish.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">The language changes the app&#x27;s menus and messages. New Bots write to you in that language unless you write to them in another one. Voice chat has its own <strong>Language</strong> setting. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/voice-chat">Voice chat with a Bot</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-reconnect-a-plugin"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-reconnect-a-plugin" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-reconnect-a-plugin" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-reconnect-a-plugin"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I reconnect a plugin?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Match what you see:</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>The connect card never shows up.</strong> A Bot shows a connect card only when a plugin needs you to sign in. Don&#x27;t wait for one. Open <strong>Plugins</strong> in the sidebar, open the plugin, and choose <strong>Authorize</strong>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>The connect card does nothing, or sign-in didn&#x27;t finish.</strong> If the card says it is waiting for authorization, choose <strong>Reopen</strong> to bring the sign-in page back. If it says authorization didn&#x27;t finish or failed, choose <strong>Retry</strong>. Finish the sign-in in your browser within about 10 minutes, or start again from the card.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>The plugin says Added, but the Bot says it&#x27;s missing.</strong> <strong>Added</strong> means the plugin is installed. It doesn&#x27;t mean you are signed in. Open <strong>Plugins</strong>, open the plugin, and check its status:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Needs auth</strong> or <strong>Disconnected</strong>: choose <strong>Authorize</strong> and finish the sign-in.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Disabled by team admin</strong>: ask your Cursor team admin to turn it on. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/connect-plugins#why-does-a-plugin-say-disabled-by-team-admin">Why does a plugin say &quot;Disabled by team admin&quot;?</a></li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Connected</strong>: send the Bot a new message and ask it to try the plugin again.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If it still doesn&#x27;t work, choose <strong>Remove</strong>, add the plugin again, and sign in. If that doesn&#x27;t help, <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/get-help">contact support</a> with the plugin name and your account email.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-add-a-second-account-for-the-same-plugin"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-add-a-second-account-for-the-same-plugin" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-add-a-second-account-for-the-same-plugin" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-add-a-second-account-for-the-same-plugin"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I add a second account for the same plugin?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Some plugins can connect more than one account, like two Notion workspaces.</p>
<ol class="mb-4 prose-ol-markers" style="list-style-type:decimal;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Open <strong>Plugins</strong> in the sidebar and open the plugin.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Under <strong>Accounts</strong>, choose <strong>Add Another Account</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Give the account a label, like work or personal.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Choose <strong>Authorize</strong> and sign in to the second account.</li>
</ol>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On the phone, choose <strong>Authorize new account</strong>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Tell the Bot which account to use by its label. For example: &quot;Use my work Notion.&quot; Every Bot on your Grok Bot account can use every connected account.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If you don&#x27;t see <strong>Add Another Account</strong>, that plugin connects one account at a time. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/connect-plugins#can-i-connect-two-gmail-accounts">Can I connect two Gmail accounts?</a></p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-sign-in-to-a-work-app-that-uses-okta-or-single-sign-on"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-sign-in-to-a-work-app-that-uses-okta-or-single-sign-on" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-sign-in-to-a-work-app-that-uses-okta-or-single-sign-on" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-sign-in-to-a-work-app-that-uses-okta-or-single-sign-on"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I sign in to a work app that uses Okta or single sign-on?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Your Bots work on their own cloud computer, not on your laptop. Fingerprint, Face ID, Windows Hello, and passkeys saved on your laptop or phone don&#x27;t work on the Bot&#x27;s computer. You sign in there yourself.</p>
<ol class="mb-4 prose-ol-markers" style="list-style-type:decimal;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Ask the Bot to open the work app.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">When it reaches the sign-in page, the Bot shows a <strong>Computer</strong> card marked <strong>Action needed</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Choose <strong>Take over</strong>. You now control the Bot&#x27;s computer.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Sign in with a method your company allows there, such as a password with a code or a push approval on your phone.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Choose <strong>I&#x27;m done</strong>. The Bot continues.</li>
</ol>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Choose <strong>Skip</strong> if you don&#x27;t want to sign in now.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On Mac and Windows, you can also use a hardware security key, such as a YubiKey, plugged into your computer. Open <strong>Settings</strong>, go to <strong>General</strong>, and under <strong>Security Key</strong> turn on <strong>Use hardware security keys</strong>. Grok Bot asks you to approve each use.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">All your Bots share one computer. After you sign in, every Bot on your account can use that sign-in until it expires. Don&#x27;t sign in to an account on the Bot&#x27;s computer if one of your Bots shouldn&#x27;t use it. When you chat with a teammate&#x27;s <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/team-bots">Team Bot</a>, it usually works on your computer too, so it can use those sign-ins.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="why-is-the-bot-asking-me-to-approve-something"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#why-is-the-bot-asking-me-to-approve-something" data-heading-base-id="why-is-the-bot-asking-me-to-approve-something" aria-label="Copy link to why-is-the-bot-asking-me-to-approve-something"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Why is the Bot asking me to approve something?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">An approval card means the Bot stopped and is waiting. The action does not run until you choose.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Auto-review</strong> checks an action before it runs and asks when it needs you. The card says <strong>Review an action</strong>.</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Allow once</strong> lets this action run this time.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Always allow</strong> lets this kind of action run later without asking. It adds a rule under <strong>Auto-review Rules</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>Deny</strong> stops this action.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Open <strong>Settings</strong>, then <strong>Bot</strong>, and use the <strong>Auto-review</strong> switch. The description there is <strong>Grok Bot checks each action before it runs and asks you first when needed.</strong> Under <strong>Auto-review Rules</strong>, write one short rule per action. <strong>Ask first</strong> wins if two rules conflict. If the switch says <strong>Required by your admin</strong>, you cannot turn it off.</p>

<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A secret card, a plugin sign-in, or a page that asks you to type a password is a different prompt. Use the secure card for secrets. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/secrets">Store secrets securely</a>.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="where-do-pending-approvals-show-up"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#where-do-pending-approvals-show-up" data-heading-base-id="where-do-pending-approvals-show-up" aria-label="Copy link to where-do-pending-approvals-show-up"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Where do pending approvals show up?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>In the chat.</strong> The approval card is in that Bot&#x27;s chat.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>In the sidebar.</strong> The Bot shows that it needs attention.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong>As a notification.</strong> If the Bot&#x27;s <strong>Notifications</strong> switch is on, you get an alert that the Bot needs you. Desktop alerts don&#x27;t show while Grok Bot is the window you&#x27;re using. The sidebar still shows them.</li>
</ul>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On the phone, allow notifications for Grok Bot in your phone settings to get alerts there. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/help/grok-bot/mobile#how-do-i-stop-phone-notifications-while-i-work-on-desktop">How do I stop phone notifications while I work on desktop?</a></p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="why-did-an-approval-expire"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#why-did-an-approval-expire" data-heading-base-id="why-did-an-approval-expire" aria-label="Copy link to why-did-an-approval-expire"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Why did an approval expire?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">An approval from a chat you are in waits for you. An approval from work that started without you, like a routine, a trigger, or a message from another Bot, expires after about 10 minutes. The card then shows <strong>Expired</strong>, and the action doesn&#x27;t run.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">To catch approvals in time:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Turn on <strong>Notifications</strong> for Bots that run routines. The switch is in the Bot&#x27;s <strong>Bot settings</strong> on desktop and in its profile on the phone.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Choose <strong>Always allow</strong> for actions you trust, so the Bot doesn&#x27;t need to ask next time. An expired card may offer <strong>Always allow this in the future</strong>.</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Ask the Bot to try the task again after an approval expires.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="how-do-i-turn-off-notifications-for-helper-bots"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#how-do-i-turn-off-notifications-for-helper-bots" data-heading-base-id="how-do-i-turn-off-notifications-for-helper-bots" aria-label="Copy link to how-do-i-turn-off-notifications-for-helper-bots"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">How do I turn off notifications for helper Bots?</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">A helper Bot is a Bot that one of your Bots created to help with its work. It shows up in your sidebar and notifies you like any other Bot.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On desktop, open the helper Bot&#x27;s chat, click its name at the top, and choose <strong>Bot settings</strong>. Turn off <strong>Notifications</strong>. The note there is <strong>Get notified when this Bot finishes or needs input</strong>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">On the phone, open the helper Bot&#x27;s chat, tap its name at the top, and turn off <strong>Notifications</strong>.</p>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">You can also hide the helper Bot. A hidden Bot doesn&#x27;t send notifications. See <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="#how-do-i-hide-a-bot-from-the-sidebar">How do I hide a Bot from the sidebar?</a></p>
